- **sr-arbiter**: SuperReview 异构多模型协同 Review 的 L4 仲裁层。读取 aggregator.py 输出的 aggregated.json，对 critical 级别 + needs_arbitration 列表做逐条复核，结合代码上下文做业务校准，输出最终 verdict (approve/request_changes/needs_human) 和 prioritized findings list。**强调严谨判断而非简单汇总。** ([heresun/SuperReview](https://github.com/heresun/SuperReview/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-arbiter.md))
- **sr-bugs**: Reviews staged git diff for bugs, logic errors, null safety issues, edge cases, and error handling problems. Launch this agent when performing staged code review. ([ben3329/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ben3329/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/staged-review/agents/sr-bugs.md))
- **sr-change-navigator**: Use this agent FIRST in a PR review, before any other reviewer. It reads the diff and PR description and produces a "reading guide" — what the change really does (intent vs. incidental churn), the business domain it touches, the entry points, the optimal order to read the files, and the risk hotspots. Its output is passed as shared context to every other reviewer so they review with intent rather than cold. Invoke when starting a /sugendran-reviews review, when orienting before reviewing an unfamiliar PR, or when a diff is large and you need to know what actually matters before reading line b… ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-change-navigator.md))
- **sr-code-reviewer**: Use this agent to review the general quality of a change — correctness, project-guideline compliance, and comment accuracy. It reads CLAUDE.md (if present) and checks the diff against it, hunts for bugs, and flags comments that contradict, mislead, or have gone stale relative to the code. Invoke when reviewing a PR for overall quality, before committing or opening a PR, or as the always-on general pass within /sugendran-reviews review. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-code-reviewer.md))
- **sr-consistency**: Reviews staged git diff for consistency with project conventions, patterns, CLAUDE.md rules, and coding standards. Launch this agent when performing staged code review. ([ben3329/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ben3329/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/staged-review/agents/sr-consistency.md))
- **sr-eng**: Council persona — Senior Engineer. Phase A author of working code that closes the next failing Acceptance Criterion(s); owner of happy-path unit tests for any new code path. Phase B participant in council ponderation. Reads canonical memory only by invoking /yoke:search-canonical-memory via the Skill tool. Never writes canonical memory. Never authors acceptance-criteria-anchored tests (that is Sr QA's anti-scope). ([iurykrieger/claude-yoke](https://github.com/iurykrieger/claude-yoke/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-eng.md) · ★2)

- **sr-game-artist**: Use this agent for visual direction, art pipeline management, and art asset specifications. - **sr-ios-dev**: Senior iOS / SwiftUI ENGINEER who implements ThemeKit components and infrastructure to spec. - **sr-orchestrator**: SuperReview 异构多模型协同 Review 的 L1 编排器。负责解析 PR/diff、风险评级、构建 context bundle、生成 5 个 Worker 专属 prompt、调用 dispatcher 并行派发外部 CLI worker、并通过 Agent tool 直接派发 CC 内部 worker (security/maintainability)。在所有 worker 完成后调用 aggregator.py 聚合，最后交给 sr-arbiter 仲裁。**只编排，不直接给最终结论。** ([heresun/SuperReview](https://github.com/heresun/SuperReview/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-orchestrator.md))
- **sr-performance**: Reviews staged git diff for performance issues including algorithmic complexity, database queries, memory usage, and resource management. - **sr-pm**: Use this agent for initial backlog creation during Discovery & Framing phase AND for bug triage when agents discover bugs during execution. This agent is the FINAL GATEKEEPER for D&F, ensuring comprehensive backlog creation from BUSINESS.md, DESIGN.md, and ARCHITECTURE.md. CRITICAL - embeds ALL context into stories so developers need nothing else. Also the DEFAULT agent authorized to create bugs -- receives DISCOVERED_BUG reports from Developer and PM-Acceptor agents, creates fully structured bugs with AC, epic placement, and dependency chain. When bug_fast_track is enabled (or story has pm-c… ([paivot-ai/paivot-graph](https://github.com/paivot-ai/paivot-graph/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-pm.md) · ★9)
- **sr-production-safety**: Use this agent to judge whether a change would deploy safely in a distributed-systems environment. It checks rolling-deploy compatibility, backward/forward schema compatibility, safe data migrations (expand-contract), idempotency and retries, partial-failure blast radius, rollback and feature-flagging, concurrency hazards, and whether new code paths are observable in production. Invoke when a PR changes APIs, schemas, migrations, background jobs, network calls, or shared state, or as the production-safety pass within /sugendran-reviews review. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-production-safety.md))
- **sr-qa**: Council persona — Senior QA. Phase A author of acceptance-criteria-anchored tests under tests/acceptance/&lt;contract-slug&gt;/, and judge of computational + inferential sensor verdicts against the binding Acceptance Criteria document's `### Validation` blocks. Phase B participant in council ponderation. Reads canonical memory only by invoking /yoke:search-canonical-memory via the Skill tool. Never writes canonical memory. Never modifies production code (that is Sr Eng's surface). ([iurykrieger/claude-yoke](https://github.com/iurykrieger/claude-yoke/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-qa.md) · ★2)

- **sr-security**: Reviews staged git diff for security vulnerabilities including injection, authentication, data exposure, and input validation issues. Launch this agent when performing staged code review. ([ben3329/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ben3329/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/staged-review/agents/sr-security.md))
- **sr-security-reviewer**: Use this agent to threat-model a change for security defects — injection, broken authentication/authorisation, secrets exposure, weak input validation, unsafe deserialization, SSRF, path traversal, crypto misuse, insecure defaults, and sensitive-data leaks in errors or responses. It reviews the change, not the whole codebase. Invoke when a PR touches auth, user input, data access, external calls, crypto, file/network I/O, or as the security pass within /sugendran-reviews review. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-security-reviewer.md))
- **sr-silent-failure-hunter**: Use this agent to find silent and masked failures in a change — errors that get swallowed, hidden, or quietly turned into a default. It hunts empty/over-broad catch blocks, ignored exceptions, fallbacks that mask a real failure, missing error logging, and errors converted to nil/null/default without justification. Invoke when a change touches error handling, adds catch/rescue/except blocks or fallback logic, or as the error-handling pass within /sugendran-reviews review when error paths changed. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-silent-failure-hunter.md))
- **sr-staff**: Council persona — Senior Staff. Phase A author of architectural verdicts grounded in canonical memory and the configured review-skill (default /review); Phase B participant in council ponderation. Reads canonical memory only by invoking /yoke:search-canonical-memory via the Skill tool. Never writes canonical memory. Never modifies code (no Write/Edit). Never invokes /ultrareview autonomously. ([iurykrieger/claude-yoke](https://github.com/iurykrieger/claude-yoke/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-staff.md) · ★2)
- **sr-synthesis**: Use this agent LAST in a PR review, after all reviewers have run. It comprehends each finding against the real code, resolves its exact line, gates out anything it cannot confirm, facilitates between reviewers (cross-applying lenses and flagging follow-up passes), and produces ONE ranked action plan — applying scope discipline, deduping, and ordering Critical to Low. It also reads the PR's existing feedback (human comments and prior reviews) so it reports what is new, marks previously-raised findings as still open rather than re-explaining them, and confirms which have been resolved. Invoke w… ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-synthesis.md))
- **sr-test-analyzer**: Use this agent to review test quality and coverage for a change. Beyond behavioural-coverage gaps, it does three things — flags tautological tests that prove nothing, checks that tests explain the business domain (not just exercise code), and derives how a human would manually verify the change then checks whether the tests cover those paths. Invoke when a PR adds or changes logic or tests, before marking a PR ready, or as the test pass within /sugendran-reviews review. ([sugendran/sugendran-codes](https://github.com/sugendran/sugendran-codes/blob/HEAD/plugins/sugendran-reviews/agents/sr-test-analyzer.md))
- **sr-type-design-analyzer**: Use this agent to review the design of types AND interfaces a change adds or modifies. Invoke when a PR introduces or changes types, data models, interfaces, or any public API surface, or as the type pass within /sugendran-reviews review. - **srk-auditor**: Read-only Shrinkage auditor — finds and evidences code-reduction candidates (dead code, duplication, speculative structure) in an assigned sweep. Produces evidence, never edits files. This is the "find WHAT to lift and shift" role — runs on the capable model because judging what is truly removable is the hard part. ([parkktech/shrinkage](https://github.com/parkktech/shrinkage/blob/HEAD/agents/srk-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **srk-surgeon**: Shrinkage surgeon — executes exactly ONE already-decided consolidation-catalog transform atomically, with a green-test gate and revert-on-red. This is the mechanical "lift and shift" role — runs on a cheaper/faster model because the judgment (what and how) was already made by the auditor; the surgeon applies a specified change and lets the test gate catch mistakes. ([parkktech/shrinkage](https://github.com/parkktech/shrinkage/blob/HEAD/agents/srk-surgeon.md) · ★1)
- **srk-verifier**: Adversarial Shrinkage verifier — tries to prove a completed reduction BROKE behavior, by re-deriving evidence independently and checking the transform's known failure modes. This is a judgment role — runs on the capable model because catching a subtle break is worth more than the tokens saved. ([parkktech/shrinkage](https://github.com/parkktech/shrinkage/blob/HEAD/agents/srk-verifier.md) · ★1)

- **srr-alerting**: The alerting and on-call seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to triage the alerts already live and to decide, per journey, when to wake someone and how the alert reaches them, then build or prepare those alerts and their routing. Not for choosing the health measure (the observability seat) or writing runbooks (the runbooks seat). ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-alerting.md))
- **srr-investigation**: The investigation and post-incident seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer when the work starts from an event (an error in hand, or an incident nothing caught). For an error in hand it first says how bad, what to do now and whom to engage. It places the event in the chain, investigates it in the logs, monitoring and code the room can reach, and says where the finding belongs (a measure, an alert, a runbook branch, or the room's map of the system), for the other seats to build. Read-only; not for fixing the code or running the incident. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-investigation.md))
- **srr-observability**: The observability seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to understand a capability's journeys and the code behind each, find who depends on it, decide what "healthy" means per journey, and build the health check and its views. Files what cannot yet be seen as instrumentation issues for the developers. Not for alerting or runbooks; those are other seats. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-observability.md))
- **srr-review**: The review seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to check one output from one discipline, cold, having made none of it, or to check the chief's proposal of the five, placement or report. It reports findings and never edits the output. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-review.md))
- **srr-runbooks**: The runbooks and incident response seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to write and assemble a capability's home (alert index, health check page, component pages, runbooks in the runbook model's form) and to run the tabletop walk that tests it. Not for choosing measures or alerts; it takes those from the other seats. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-runbooks.md))
- **srr-steward**: The steward of the service reliability room, its practice owner. Launched by the room's chief engineer after each report, with what the run recorded, or when the person says the room got something wrong, or directly by the person ("update the room", "add a seat"). It gathers the person's reactions, the misses and second-pass findings, proposes a change to the room's skill or seats when they add up to one, and, once the person approves and an independent review passes, applies it to this copy of the room and logs it in CHANGES.md. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-steward.md))
- **srr-transition**: The service transition seat of the service reliability room, held from the side of the team that runs production. Launched first by the room's chief engineer to find what either team has already recorded and what is live with too little behind it, to turn what only the development team knows into specific requests with proposals attached, and to flag steps an operator could not follow. Not a gate and not a handover checklist; it drafts nothing the other seats own. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-transition.md))

Activate when evaluating technology choices for a new system or validating an existing stack. ([Nerfherder16/BrickLayer](https://github.com/Nerfherder16/BrickLayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack-validator.md))
- **stack-verifier**: Runs the full green sweep across backend and frontend (lint, typecheck, build, unit, e2e) and reports only what failed. Use before declaring work done, before a commit or PR, or whenever the user asks whether the stack is still green. Do NOT use to fix failures — it reports, the caller fixes. ([NXT-ANH/my-project](https://github.com/NXT-ANH/my-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack-verifier.md))
- **stack-verifier**: Re-checks library versions and APIs used by the Gloam spec and reports breaking changes with minimal verified snippets. Use for Phase 0 task R3 and when an API behaves unexpectedly. ([XiniDev/Gloam](https://github.com/XiniDev/Gloam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack-verifier.md))
- **stack-watcher**: Watches releases, changelogs and security advisories of the dependencies in doc/STACK.md against the features we actually use, and tells the orchestrator when an update is worth it. Full definition in doc/TEAM.md; report per doc/SUBAGENTS.md §5. ([juannito/harness-agents](https://github.com/juannito/harness-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/stack-watcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **stack-worker**: Implements one scoped task of the lakehouse stack end-to-end (a compose service, an ingest script, a set of dbt models, a Cube schema, a Makefile target) and validates it locally. Use for any non-trivial implementation task whose SCOPE can be stated as a list of paths. ([jhorlima/sleipnir](https://github.com/jhorlima/sleipnir/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack-worker.md))
- **stack-writer**: Post semanal do Radar de Notícias Stack (#11184, skill `/diaria-stack`): escreve o texto PRÓPRIO do post para o público da Stack (alunos dos cursos de Lovable, Cursor, Claude Code, n8n e Replit) — a abertura, a descrição de cada item de Lançamentos, Use melhor e Radar, no ângulo de quem constrói, e a descrição do vídeo da semana (só fatos do destaque comentado, #12549). Lê o insumo de `circle-stack-copy.ts --manifest` e grava `_internal/circle-stack-copy.json`. Destaques ficam como na diária. Não publica nada. ([vjpixel/diaria-studio](https://github.com/vjpixel/diaria-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack-writer.md) · ★1)
- **stack:backend-builder**: Implements backend and other non-frontend code from a PRD or scoped task using TDD. Runs the engine and mode it is handed (superpowers or a self-contained loop; full or quick). Never opens a PR. ([browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core](https://github.com/browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack%3Abackend-builder.md) · ★1)

- **stack:dev-architect**: Per-module grounding + boundary agent for stack:tech-spec. Reads one module's own .claude/rules, .claude/knowledge, and CLAUDE.md; cites the closest existing analogous pattern (verified by file + symbol, never line numbers, never invented); reports the module's cross-module boundary needs (what it consumes/produces at the interface). Does NOT design the module's internals - that belongs to the shared ideation session (uncovered non-frontend) or to Plumb (frontend). ([browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core](https://github.com/browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack%3Adev-architect.md) · ★1)

Never mutates, never posts — diagnostic only. Dispatched by stack:debug-alert. ([browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core](https://github.com/browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack%3Aincident-investigator.md) · ★1)

([browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core](https://github.com/browserstack/a11y-engine-axe-core/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stack%3Aqa-test-author.md) · ★1)

([KarmaCummunity/KC](https://github.com/KarmaCummunity/KC/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/STACK_MAP.md) · ★1)

- **stacked-prs**: A stack is a chain of pull requests (PRs). Each PR has the branch of the PR below it as its base. The bottom PR has `main` as its base. ([OpenDustpan/OpenDustpan](https://github.com/OpenDustpan/OpenDustpan/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/stacked-prs.md))
- **stacked-prs**: A **stack** is a chain of open PRs, each based on the branch below it, with the bottom one based on `main`. GitHub shows the chain on every PR in it, and each PR's diff holds only its own commits, so the user reviews them one layer at a time. The `gh stack` extension (`github/gh-stack`) is installed; `gh stack &lt;command&gt; --help` is the full reference. ([StanleyGoldman/email-ops](https://github.com/StanleyGoldman/email-ops/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/stacked-prs.md))

- **stage-reviewer**: Ревьюер одного этапа задачи в цикле этапа (docs/stage-cycle.md) - сверяет незакоммиченный дифф этапа (форма, код, тесты) со спекой, планом и правилами проекта, сам запускает диагностику EDT и тесты этапа и выносит вердикт по формату. - **stage-runner**: Run a single phased-plan stage end-to-end by invoking /bytheslice:sell-slice for that specific stage. The actual delivery logic lives in sell-slice; this agent is a thin wrapper that loads the right context, runs the inner skill, and returns its structured result. Dispatched one-at-a-time by run-the-day (the experimental autonomous orchestrator). - **stage-session**: A lifecycle stage session dispatched by the iteration lead (lifecycle-kit/templates/lead.md).
